3217: function clipRoutePathToFeature(path,feature){
3218:   const dense=densifyRoutePath(path,0.025),parts=[];let current=[];
3219:   for(const p of dense){
3220:     if(pointInGeoFeature([+p.lng,+p.lat],feature))current.push({lat:+p.lat,lng:+p.lng});
3221:     else{if(current.length>=2)parts.push(current);current=[]}
3222:   }
3223:   if(current.length>=2)parts.push(current);

3226: function thinRoutePath(path,maxPts=90){
3227:   const pts=(path||[]).filter(p=>Number.isFinite(+p.lat)&&Number.isFinite(+p.lng));
3228:   if(pts.length<=maxPts)return pts.map(p=>({lat:+p.lat,lng:+p.lng}));
3229:   const out=[];
3230:   for(let i=0;i<maxPts;i++){
3231:     const idx=Math.round(i*(pts.length-1)/(maxPts-1));
3232:     if(!out.length||idx!==out[out.length-1]._idx)out.push({lat:+pts[idx].lat,lng:+pts[idx].lng,_idx:idx});
3233:   }
3234:   return out.map(({_idx,...p})=>p);
3235: }
3236: function bufferedRoutePolygon(path,radiusKm=RURAL_ROUTE_MATCH_KM){
3237:   const pts=thinRoutePath(path,90);if(pts.length<2)return [];
3238:   const left=[],right=[];
3239:   for(let i=0;i<pts.length;i++){
3240:     const a=pts[Math.max(0,i-1)],b=pts[Math.min(pts.length-1,i+1)];
3241:     const dx=(+b.lng-+a.lng)*88,dy=(+b.lat-+a.lat)*111,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
3242:     const nx=-dy/len,ny=dx/len;
3243:     left.push({lat:+pts[i].lat+(ny*radiusKm/111),lng:+pts[i].lng+(nx*radiusKm/88)});
3244:     right.push({lat:+pts[i].lat-(ny*radiusKm/111),lng:+pts[i].lng-(nx*radiusKm/88)});
3245:   }
3246:   return [...left,...right.reverse()];
3247: }

3301: async function buildGridServiceZonesForRegion(type,region,groups){
3302:   const b=featureBounds(region.feature);if(!b||!groups.length)return;
3303:   const rural=RI_LAYER_DISTRICTS.has(region.district);
3304:   if(groups.length===1){
3305:     wholeFeatureZone(type,region.feature,{district:region.district,riName:region.riName||'',vehicle:groups[0].vehicle,provider:groups[0].provider||routeCompany(groups[0].vehicle),days:groups[0].days,groupKey:groups[0].key,automatic:true,rural});
3306:     return;
3307:   }
3308:   const latStep=.00175,lngStep=.00205;
3309:   const ny=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((b.maxLat-b.minLat)/latStep));
3310:   const nx=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((b.maxLng-b.minLng)/lngStep));
3311:   const grid=Array.from({length:ny},()=>Array(nx).fill(null));
3312:   const groupByKey=new Map(groups.map(g=>[g.key,g]));
3313: 
3314:   // 1) Base classification by nearest source route.
3315:   for(let iy=0;iy<ny;iy++){
3316:     const lat0=b.minLat+iy*latStep,lat1=Math.min(b.maxLat,lat0+latStep),lat=(lat0+lat1)/2;
3317:     for(let ix=0;ix<nx;ix++){
3318:       const lng0=b.minLng+ix*lngStep,lng1=Math.min(b.maxLng,lng0+lngStep),lng=(lng0+lng1)/2;
3319:       if(!pointInGeoFeature([lng,lat],region.feature))continue;
3320:       const cls=classifyServiceGroups(groups,lat,lng);if(!cls)continue;
3321:       grid[iy][ix]={ix,iy,key:cls.group.key,ambiguous:cls.ambiguous,estimated:false,group:cls.group,lng0,lng1,lat0,lat1};
3322:     }
3323:     if(iy%8===0)await sleepMs(0);
3324:   }
3325: 
3326:   function dominantNeighborForBoundary(iy,ix,cell){
3327:     for(const radius of [1,2,3]){
3328:       const counts=new Map();let total=0;
3329:       for(let dy=-radius;dy<=radius;dy++)for(let dx=-radius;dx<=radius;dx++){
3330:         if(!dx&&!dy)continue;
3331:         if(Math.max(Math.abs(dx),Math.abs(dy))!==radius)continue;
3332:         const n=grid[iy+dy]?.[ix+dx];
3333:         if(!n||n.ambiguous)continue;
3334:         counts.set(n.key,(counts.get(n.key)||0)+1);total++;
3335:       }
3336:       if(!counts.size)continue;
3337:       const ranked=[...counts.entries()].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]||(a[0]===cell.key?-1:(b[0]===cell.key?1:String(a[0]).localeCompare(String(b[0]),'ko'))));
3338:       const top=ranked[0],second=ranked[1];
3339:       if(!second||top[1]>second[1])return {key:top[0],support:top[1],total,ratio:top[1]/Math.max(1,total)};
3340:     }
3341:     const counts=new Map();let total=0;
3342:     for(let dy=-3;dy<=3;dy++)for(let dx=-3;dx<=3;dx++){
3343:       if(!dx&&!dy)continue;
3344:       const n=grid[iy+dy]?.[ix+dx];if(!n)continue;
3345:       counts.set(n.key,(counts.get(n.key)||0)+1);total++;
3346:     }
3347:     if(counts.size){
3348:       const ranked=[...counts.entries()].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]||(a[0]===cell.key?-1:(b[0]===cell.key?1:String(a[0]).localeCompare(String(b[0]),'ko'))));
3349:       const top=ranked[0];return {key:top[0],support:top[1],total,ratio:top[1]/Math.max(1,total)};
3350:     }
3351:     return {key:cell.key,support:0,total:0,ratio:0};
3352:   }
3353: 
3354:   // 2) Boundary cells inherit the locally dominant neighboring route but stay marked estimated.
3355:   for(let iy=0;iy<ny;iy++)for(let ix=0;ix<nx;ix++){
3356:     const cell=grid[iy][ix];if(!cell?.ambiguous)continue;
3357:     const choice=dominantNeighborForBoundary(iy,ix,cell);
3358:     const chosen=groupByKey.get(choice.key)||cell.group;
3359:     cell.key=chosen.key;cell.group=chosen;cell.ambiguous=false;cell.estimated=true;
3360:     cell.neighborRatio=choice.ratio;cell.neighborSupport=choice.support;cell.neighborTotal=choice.total;
3361:   }
3362: 
3363:   // 3) Merge adjacent cells into large display blocks.  First merge each row, then extend
3364:   // identical runs vertically.  Any remaining touching blocks have no internal stroke, so
3365:   // they read visually as one service area rather than a checkerboard of cells.
3366:   const finished=[];let active=new Map();
3367:   for(let iy=0;iy<ny;iy++){
3368:     const runs=mergedRowRuns(grid[iy].filter(Boolean));
3369:     const next=new Map();
3370:     for(const run of runs){
3371:       const id=`${run.sig}|${run.x0}|${run.x1}`;
3372:       const prev=active.get(id);
3373:       if(prev&&prev.iy1===iy-1){
3374:         prev.iy1=iy;prev.lat1=run.lat1;
3375:         if(prev.estimated){
3376:           prev.neighborSupport+=(+run.neighborSupport||0);
3377:           prev.neighborTotal+=(+run.neighborTotal||0);
3378:           prev.neighborRatio=prev.neighborTotal?prev.neighborSupport/prev.neighborTotal:(+prev.neighborRatio||0);
3379:         }
3380:         next.set(id,prev);
3381:       }else{
3382:         next.set(id,{...run,iy0:iy,iy1:iy});
3383:       }
3384:     }
3385:     for(const [id,rect] of active)if(!next.has(id))finished.push(rect);
3386:     active=next;
3387:   }
3388:   for(const rect of active.values())finished.push(rect);
3389: 
3390:   for(const rect of finished){
3391:     const left=b.minLng+rect.x0*lngStep,right=Math.min(b.maxLng,b.minLng+(rect.x1+1)*lngStep);
3392:     const bottom=b.minLat+rect.iy0*latStep,top=Math.min(b.maxLat,b.minLat+(rect.iy1+1)*latStep);
3393:     addServiceZonePolygon(type,[{lat:bottom,lng:left},{lat:bottom,lng:right},{lat:top,lng:right},{lat:top,lng:left}],{
3394:       district:region.district,riName:region.riName||'',vehicle:rect.group.vehicle,provider:rect.group.provider||routeCompany(rect.group.vehicle),days:rect.group.days,groupKey:rect.group.key,
3395:       ambiguous:false,estimated:!!rect.estimated,neighborRatio:+rect.neighborRatio||0,neighborSupport:+rect.neighborSupport||0,neighborTotal:+rect.neighborTotal||0,automatic:true,rural,gridMerged:true
3396:     });
3397:   }
3398: }

1523: function serviceZoneOverlayMatch(type,ctx){
1524:   const district=canonicalDistrict(ctx?.legalEmd||ctx?.district||'');
1525:   const riName=normalizeRi(ctx?.legalRi||'');
1526:   const lat=+ctx?.lat,lng=+ctx?.lng;
1527:   if(!Number.isFinite(lat)||!Number.isFinite(lng))return null;
1528:   const manual=manualZoneMatch(type,lat,lng);
1529:   if(manual)return {vehicle:manual.vehicle,provider:manual.provider||routeCompany(manual.vehicle),days:[...(manual.days||[])],distanceKm:Infinity,matchBasis:'manual-zone',zoneStatus:'manual',matchedRoads:[],note:manual.note||'',district,riName};
1530:   const hits=(serviceZoneOverlays[type]||[]).filter(z=>{
1531:     if(z.manual)return false;
1532:     if(z.district&&canonicalDistrict(z.district)!==district)return false;
1533:     if(riName && normalizeRi(z.riName||'')!==riName)return false;
1534:     return pointInZonePolygon(lat,lng,z);
1535:   });
1536:   if(!hits.length)return null;
1537:   hits.sort((a,b)=>(a.ambiguous?2:(a.estimated?1:0))-(b.ambiguous?2:(b.estimated?1:0)));
1538:   const z=hits[0];
1539:   return {vehicle:z.vehicle,vehicles:[...(z.vehicles||[])],provider:z.provider||routeCompany(z.vehicle),plate:z.plate||'',days:[...(z.days||[])],distanceKm:Infinity,matchBasis:'displayed-zone',zoneStatus:z.estimated?'estimated':(z.ambiguous?'boundary':'inferred'),estimated:!!z.estimated,neighborRatio:Number.isFinite(+z.neighborRatio)?+z.neighborRatio:0,neighborSupport:+z.neighborSupport||0,neighborTotal:+z.neighborTotal||0,matchedRoads:[],district,riName,riGrouped:!!z.riGrouped,contractLayer:!!z.contractLayer,routeBuffer:!!z.routeBuffer,noSchedule:!!z.noSchedule,groupOutline:!!z.groupOutline,gridMerged:!!z.gridMerged,polygonized:!!z.polygonized};

1552: function geoPointKm(lat,lng){
1553:   return {x:+lng*88,y:+lat*111};
1554: }
1555: function pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,a,b){
1556:   if(!a||!b)return Infinity;
1557:   const p=geoPointKm(lat,lng), p1=geoPointKm(a.lat,a.lng), p2=geoPointKm(b.lat,b.lng);
1558:   const vx=p2.x-p1.x, vy=p2.y-p1.y;
1559:   const wx=p.x-p1.x, wy=p.y-p1.y;
1560:   const vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
1561:   let t=vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0;
1562:   t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
1563:   return Math.hypot(p.x-(p1.x+t*vx),p.y-(p1.y+t*vy));
1564: }

1567: function routeItemDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1568:   // 주소 20m 직접매칭은 실제 표시 수거선만 사용합니다.
1569:   let best=Infinity;
1570:   for(const seg of (item?.segments||[])){
1571:     for(let i=1;i<seg.length;i++){
1572:       best=Math.min(best,pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,seg[i-1],seg[i]));
1573:     }
1574:   }
1575:   return best;
1576: }

1552: function geoPointKm(lat,lng){
1553:   return {x:+lng*88,y:+lat*111};
1554: }
1555: function pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,a,b){
1556:   if(!a||!b)return Infinity;
1557:   const p=geoPointKm(lat,lng), p1=geoPointKm(a.lat,a.lng), p2=geoPointKm(b.lat,b.lng);
1558:   const vx=p2.x-p1.x, vy=p2.y-p1.y;
1559:   const wx=p.x-p1.x, wy=p.y-p1.y;
1560:   const vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
1561:   let t=vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0;
1562:   t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
1563:   return Math.hypot(p.x-(p1.x+t*vx),p.y-(p1.y+t*vy));
1564: }
1565: const MAX_ADDRESS_ROUTE_DISTANCE_KM=0.020;
1566: function routeMatchLimitKm(district){return RURAL_INFERRED_ROUTE_DISTRICTS.has(canonicalDistrict(district))?RURAL_ROUTE_MATCH_KM:MAX_ADDRESS_ROUTE_DISTANCE_KM} // 주소점에서 실제 표시 수거노선 20m 이내만 수거일정으로 인정
1567: function routeItemDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1568:   // 주소 20m 직접매칭은 실제 표시 수거선만 사용합니다.
1569:   let best=Infinity;
1570:   for(const seg of (item?.segments||[])){
1571:     for(let i=1;i<seg.length;i++){
1572:       best=Math.min(best,pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,seg[i-1],seg[i]));
1573:     }
1574:   }
1575:   return best;
1576: }
1577: function serviceZoneEvidenceDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1578:   // ① 수거권역 추정에는 선으로 만들기 어려운 시설·마을명도 '숨은 근거점'으로 활용합니다.
1579:   // 이 점들은 지도에 원/마커로 표시하지 않으며, 주소 20m 직접매칭에도 사용하지 않습니다.
1580:   let best=routeItemDistanceKm(item,lat,lng);
1581:   for(const p of (item?.zoneEvidencePoints||[])){
1582:     if(!Number.isFinite(+p.lat)||!Number.isFinite(+p.lng))continue;
1583:     best=Math.min(best,routePointDistance({lat:+lat,lng:+lng},{lat:+p.lat,lng:+p.lng}));
1584:   }

1590: async function mapWithConcurrency(items,limit,worker){
1591:   const out=new Array(items.length);
1592:   let cursor=0;
1593:   async function run(){
1594:     while(true){
1595:       const i=cursor++;
1596:       if(i>=items.length)return;
1597:       try{out[i]=await worker(items[i],i)}
1598:       catch(e){console.warn('인접 수거노선 준비 실패',items[i],e);out[i]=null}
1599:     }
1600:   }
1601:   await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(limit,items.length)},()=>run()));
1602:   return out;
1603: }

1552: function geoPointKm(lat,lng){
1553:   return {x:+lng*88,y:+lat*111};
1554: }
1555: function pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,a,b){
1556:   if(!a||!b)return Infinity;
1557:   const p=geoPointKm(lat,lng), p1=geoPointKm(a.lat,a.lng), p2=geoPointKm(b.lat,b.lng);
1558:   const vx=p2.x-p1.x, vy=p2.y-p1.y;
1559:   const wx=p.x-p1.x, wy=p.y-p1.y;
1560:   const vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
1561:   let t=vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0;
1562:   t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
1563:   return Math.hypot(p.x-(p1.x+t*vx),p.y-(p1.y+t*vy));
1564: }

1590: async function mapWithConcurrency(items,limit,worker){
1591:   const out=new Array(items.length);
1592:   let cursor=0;
1593:   async function run(){
1594:     while(true){
1595:       const i=cursor++;
1596:       if(i>=items.length)return;
1597:       try{out[i]=await worker(items[i],i)}
1598:       catch(e){console.warn('인접 수거노선 준비 실패',items[i],e);out[i]=null}
1599:     }
1600:   }
1601:   await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(limit,items.length)},()=>run()));
1602:   return out;
1603: }

3243:     left.push({lat:+pts[i].lat+(ny*radiusKm/111),lng:+pts[i].lng+(nx*radiusKm/88)});
3244:     right.push({lat:+pts[i].lat-(ny*radiusKm/111),lng:+pts[i].lng-(nx*radiusKm/88)});
3245:   }
3246:   return [...left,...right.reverse()];
3247: }

3301: async function buildGridServiceZonesForRegion(type,region,groups){
3302:   const b=featureBounds(region.feature);if(!b||!groups.length)return;
3303:   const rural=RI_LAYER_DISTRICTS.has(region.district);
3304:   if(groups.length===1){
3305:     wholeFeatureZone(type,region.feature,{district:region.district,riName:region.riName||'',vehicle:groups[0].vehicle,provider:groups[0].provider||routeCompany(groups[0].vehicle),days:groups[0].days,groupKey:groups[0].key,automatic:true,rural});
3306:     return;
3307:   }
3308:   const latStep=.00175,lngStep=.00205;
3309:   const ny=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((b.maxLat-b.minLat)/latStep));
3310:   const nx=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((b.maxLng-b.minLng)/lngStep));
3311:   const grid=Array.from({length:ny},()=>Array(nx).fill(null));
3312:   const groupByKey=new Map(groups.map(g=>[g.key,g]));
3313: 
3314:   // 1) Base classification by nearest source route.
3315:   for(let iy=0;iy<ny;iy++){
3316:     const lat0=b.minLat+iy*latStep,lat1=Math.min(b.maxLat,lat0+latStep),lat=(lat0+lat1)/2;
3317:     for(let ix=0;ix<nx;ix++){
3318:       const lng0=b.minLng+ix*lngStep,lng1=Math.min(b.maxLng,lng0+lngStep),lng=(lng0+lng1)/2;
3319:       if(!pointInGeoFeature([lng,lat],region.feature))continue;
3320:       const cls=classifyServiceGroups(groups,lat,lng);if(!cls)continue;
3321:       grid[iy][ix]={ix,iy,key:cls.group.key,ambiguous:cls.ambiguous,estimated:false,group:cls.group,lng0,lng1,lat0,lat1};
3322:     }
3323:     if(iy%8===0)await sleepMs(0);
3324:   }
3325: 
3326:   function dominantNeighborForBoundary(iy,ix,cell){
3327:     for(const radius of [1,2,3]){
3328:       const counts=new Map();let total=0;
3329:       for(let dy=-radius;dy<=radius;dy++)for(let dx=-radius;dx<=radius;dx++){
3330:         if(!dx&&!dy)continue;
3331:         if(Math.max(Math.abs(dx),Math.abs(dy))!==radius)continue;
3332:         const n=grid[iy+dy]?.[ix+dx];
3333:         if(!n||n.ambiguous)continue;
3334:         counts.set(n.key,(counts.get(n.key)||0)+1);total++;
3335:       }
3336:       if(!counts.size)continue;
3337:       const ranked=[...counts.entries()].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]||(a[0]===cell.key?-1:(b[0]===cell.key?1:String(a[0]).localeCompare(String(b[0]),'ko'))));
3338:       const top=ranked[0],second=ranked[1];
3339:       if(!second||top[1]>second[1])return {key:top[0],support:top[1],total,ratio:top[1]/Math.max(1,total)};
3340:     }
3341:     const counts=new Map();let total=0;
3342:     for(let dy=-3;dy<=3;dy++)for(let dx=-3;dx<=3;dx++){
3343:       if(!dx&&!dy)continue;
3344:       const n=grid[iy+dy]?.[ix+dx];if(!n)continue;
3345:       counts.set(n.key,(counts.get(n.key)||0)+1);total++;
3346:     }
3347:     if(counts.size){
3348:       const ranked=[...counts.entries()].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]||(a[0]===cell.key?-1:(b[0]===cell.key?1:String(a[0]).localeCompare(String(b[0]),'ko'))));
3349:       const top=ranked[0];return {key:top[0],support:top[1],total,ratio:top[1]/Math.max(1,total)};
3350:     }
3351:     return {key:cell.key,support:0,total:0,ratio:0};
3352:   }
3353: 
3354:   // 2) Boundary cells inherit the locally dominant neighboring route but stay marked estimated.
3355:   for(let iy=0;iy<ny;iy++)for(let ix=0;ix<nx;ix++){
3356:     const cell=grid[iy][ix];if(!cell?.ambiguous)continue;
3357:     const choice=dominantNeighborForBoundary(iy,ix,cell);
3358:     const chosen=groupByKey.get(choice.key)||cell.group;
3359:     cell.key=chosen.key;cell.group=chosen;cell.ambiguous=false;cell.estimated=true;
3360:     cell.neighborRatio=choice.ratio;cell.neighborSupport=choice.support;cell.neighborTotal=choice.total;
3361:   }
3362: 
3363:   // 3) Merge adjacent cells into large display blocks.  First merge each row, then extend
3364:   // identical runs vertically.  Any remaining touching blocks have no internal stroke, so
3365:   // they read visually as one service area rather than a checkerboard of cells.
3366:   const finished=[];let active=new Map();
3367:   for(let iy=0;iy<ny;iy++){
3368:     const runs=mergedRowRuns(grid[iy].filter(Boolean));
3369:     const next=new Map();
3370:     for(const run of runs){
3371:       const id=`${run.sig}|${run.x0}|${run.x1}`;
3372:       const prev=active.get(id);
3373:       if(prev&&prev.iy1===iy-1){
3374:         prev.iy1=iy;prev.lat1=run.lat1;
3375:         if(prev.estimated){
3376:           prev.neighborSupport+=(+run.neighborSupport||0);
3377:           prev.neighborTotal+=(+run.neighborTotal||0);
3378:           prev.neighborRatio=prev.neighborTotal?prev.neighborSupport/prev.neighborTotal:(+prev.neighborRatio||0);

282: function contractIncombustibleRowsFor(ctx){
283:   const district=canonicalDistrict(ctx?.legalEmd||ctx?.district||'');

287: function contractorIncombustibleSummary(ctx){
288:   const rows=contractIncombustibleRowsFor(ctx);if(!rows.length)return '';
289:   const exactTarget=contractAddressNorm(ctx?.address||'');
290:   const exact=rows.filter(x=>{
291:     if(!exactTarget)return false;
292:     return contractAddressCandidates(x.address).some(k=>exactTarget.includes(k)||k.includes(exactTarget));
293:   });
294:   const use=exact.length?exact:rows;
295:   const daySet=new Set();use.forEach(x=>(x.days||[]).forEach(d=>daySet.add(d)));
296:   const days=DAYS.filter(d=>daySet.has(d));
297:   if(!days.length)return '';
298:   const provider=use[0]?.provider||'';
299:   const note=(provider==='제일환경'&&canonicalDistrict(ctx?.legalEmd||ctx?.district)==='우암동')
300:     ? ' (원자료상 물량에 따라 화·목 탄력수거 표기)'
301:     : '';
302:   return `<br><strong>불연성:</strong> ${days.join(' · ')}${note}`;
303: }
304: function contractExactScheduleMatch(type,ctx){
305:   const district=canonicalDistrict(ctx?.legalEmd||ctx?.district||'');
306:   const target=contractAddressNorm(ctx?.address||'');
307:   if(!target)return null;
308:   let best=null,bestLen=0;
309: 
310:   // 대행업체 원본 차량×요일 노선표의 주소목록을 직접 검색주소와 대조합니다.
311:   // 별도 특수지점만 보던 기존 로직 때문에 원본 일정이 있어도 '요일 확인 필요'로 빠지던 문제를 보완합니다.
312:   for(const r of (CONTRACT_ROUTES[type]||[])){
313:     if(!(r.districts||[]).some(d=>canonicalDistrict(d)===district))continue;
314:     let localBestLen=0,matchedAddress='';
315:     const matchedDays=[];
316:     for(const [day,raw] of Object.entries(r.days||{})){
317:       for(const part of String(raw||'').split(/[,/]/)){
318:         const key=contractAddressNorm(part);
319:         if(key.length<5)continue;
320:         const score=Math.min(key.length,target.length);
321:         if((target.includes(key)||key.includes(target))&&score>=5){
322:           if(!matchedDays.includes(day))matchedDays.push(day);
323:           if(score>localBestLen){localBestLen=score;matchedAddress=part.trim()}
324:         }
325:       }
326:     }
327:     if(matchedDays.length&&localBestLen>bestLen){
328:       const meta=contractRouteMeta(r.vehicle)||{};
329:       best={
330:         provider:r.provider||meta.provider||'',
331:         vehicle:r.plate||meta.plate||r.vehicle||'',
332:         days:DAYS.filter(d=>matchedDays.includes(d)),
333:         address:matchedAddress,
334:         housing:r.housing||meta.housing||'single',
335:         type
336:       };
337:       bestLen=localBestLen;
338:     }
339:   }
340: 
341:   // 특수주소 직접매칭은 그대로 유지하고, 더 구체적인 주소가 있으면 우선합니다.
342:   const rows=[];
343:   if(type==='general')rows.push(...CONTRACT_APARTMENTS.map(x=>({...x,housing:'apartment',type:'general'})));
344:   rows.push(...CONTRACT_SPECIAL_POINTS.filter(x=>x.type===type).map(x=>({...x,housing:'single'})));
345:   for(const x of rows){
346:     if(canonicalDistrict(x.district)!==district)continue;
347:     for(const key of contractAddressCandidates(x.address)){
348:       const score=Math.min(key.length,target.length);
349:       if((target.includes(key)||key.includes(target))&&score>bestLen){best=x;bestLen=score}
350:     }
351:   }
352:   return best;
353: }

356:   const phone=CONTRACT_OPERATOR_INFO[row.provider]?.phone||'';const place=[ctx?.legalEmd||ctx?.district,ctx?.legalRi].filter(Boolean).join(' ');const name=row.name?` (${escapeHtml(row.name)})`:'';
357:   document.getElementById(type+'Detail').innerHTML=`<div class="verified">${place?`<strong>판정지역:</strong> ${escapeHtml(place)}<br>`:''}<strong>수거방식:</strong> 대행수거<br><strong>수거업체:</strong> ${escapeHtml(row.provider)}${phone?' · '+phone:''}<br><strong>차량:</strong> ${escapeHtml(row.vehicle||'확인')}<br><strong>수거일:</strong> ${days.join(' · ')}${row.time&&row.time!=='"'?`<br><strong>수거시간:</strong> ${escapeHtml(row.time)}`:''}<br><strong>원자료 위치:</strong> ${escapeHtml(row.address||'')}${name}${type==='general'?contractorIncombustibleSummary(ctx):''}<br><span style="color:#6b7280">※ 대행업체 수거노선표의 해당 주소 행과 직접 매칭한 일정입니다.</span></div>`;

1754: async function nearestRouteMatchesForAddress(type,ctx){
1755:   const district=canonicalDistrict(ctx?.legalEmd||ctx?.district||'');
1756:   if(!DONG_ROUTE_DISTRICTS.has(district)&&!RURAL_INFERRED_ROUTE_DISTRICTS.has(district))return null;

1764:   const addressRoads=extractAddressRoadNames(ctx.address);
1765:

1767:   const measures=specs.map(spec=>{
1768:     const item=itemMap.get(`${spec.vehicle}|${spec.day}`)||null;
1769:     const distanceKm=item?routeItemDistanceKm(item,+ctx.lat,+ctx.lng):Infinity;
1770:     const roads=matchedAddressRoads(spec,addressRoads);
1771:     return {spec,item,distanceKm,roads,roadHit:roads.length>0};
1772:   });
1773: 
1774:   // 주소점에서 실제 표시 수거선까지 동지역 20m, 내수·북이 30m 이내인 노선만 후보로 인정합니다.
1775:   const eligible=measures.filter(m=>Number.isFinite(m.distanceKm)&&m.distanceKm<=maxRouteKm);
1776:   if(!eligible.length)return [];
1777: 
1778:   // 1순위: 허용거리 이내이면서 주소 도로명도 원본 코스에 직접 일치.
1779:   const exact=eligible.filter(m=>m.roadHit);
1780:   if(exact.length){
1781:     const byVehicle=new Map();
1782:     for(const m of exact){
1783:       if(!byVehicle.has(m.spec.vehicle))byVehicle.set(m.spec.vehicle,[]);
1784:       byVehicle.get(m.spec.vehicle).push(m);
1785:     }
1786:     const ranked=[...byVehicle.entries()].map(([vehicle,rows])=>({
1787:       vehicle,
1788:       rows,
1789:       minDist:Math.min(...rows.map(r=>r.distanceKm)),
1790:       roadHits:new Set(rows.flatMap(r=>r.roads)).size
1791:     })).sort((a,b)=>
1792:       a.minDist-b.minDist ||
1793:       b.roadHits-a.roadHits ||
1794:       b.rows.length-a.rows.length ||
1795:       a.vehicle.localeCompare(b.vehicle,'ko')
1796:     );

1798:     const winner=ranked[0];

1800:     // 동일 차량이라도 허용거리 안에 실제 선이 있는 요일만 일정에 포함합니다.

691: function directVehicleDaysForDistrict(type,district){
692:   const out={};
693:   for(const route of (DIRECT_ROUTES[type]||[])){
694:     const days=[];
695:     for(const day of Object.keys(route.days||{})){
696:       if(inferredRegionsFor(type,route.vehicle,day).includes(district))days.push(day);
697:     }
698:     if(days.length)out[route.vehicle]=DAYS.filter(d=>days.includes(d));
699:   }
700:   return out;
701: }
702: function directRegionDays(type,district){
703:   const set=new Set();
704:   Object.values(directVehicleDaysForDistrict(type,district)).forEach(days=>days.forEach(d=>set.add(d)));
705:   return DAYS.filter(d=>set.has(d));
706: }

719: function groupedScheduleForDistrict(type,district){
720:   const byDay={};
721:   const add=(day,key)=>{
722:     if(!byDay[day])byDay[day]=[];
723:     if(!byDay[day].some(x=>x.key===key))byDay[day].push({key,label:providerDisplay(key)});

761: function routeNorm(s){return String(s||'').toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,'').replace(/[·.()@-]/g,'')}
762: // 코스표의 축약 도로명을 실제 도로명 단위로 풀어냅니다.
763: // 예) 공항로70,64,58,52번길 → 공항로70번길, 공항로64번길, ...
764: //     밀레니엄1,2,3,4로 → 밀레니엄1로, 밀레니엄2로, ...
765: function expandRouteShorthand(text){
766:   let src=String(text||'');
767:   // 원본 특수 약칭을 먼저 풀어 순서가 깨지지 않게 합니다.
768:   src=src.replace(/정상8,9통/g,'정상8통,정상9통');
769:   src=src.replace(/오창중,고/g,'오창중,오창고');
770:   src=src.replace(/([가-힣A-Za-z0-9]+(?:대로|로|길))(\d+(?:-\d+)?(?:,\d+(?:-\d+)?)+)번길/g,(m,base,nums)=>
771:     nums.split(',').map(n=>`${base}${n}번길`).join(',')
772:   );
773:   src=src.replace(/([가-힣A-Za-z]+)(\d+(?:,\d+)+)로(?=\s*(?:일대|간선|,|\n|$))/g,(m,base,nums)=>
774:     nums.split(',').map(n=>`${base}${n}로`).join(',')
775:   );
776:   return src;
777: }
778: function cleanRouteTerm(raw){
779:   let t=String(raw||'')
780:     .replace(/^(가연|재활용|불연|대\.?형)\s*-/,'')
781:     .replace(/\b(전\s*코스\s*수거|전코스\s*수거)\b/g,'')
782:     .replace(/(일대|일원|간선|수거)$/g,'')
783:     .trim();
784:   if(!t || /^\d+$/.test(t) || t.length<2)return '';
785:   if(/^(원룸지역|상가지역|주거지역|공동주택|단독주택|주요간선도로|주요간선|원룸|상가)$/.test(t))return '';
786:   return t;
787: }
788: function routeTerms(text){

792:   // 예: 대신의원(율천북로) → 대신의원 → 율천북로

797:       const outer=cleanRouteTerm(u.replace(/\([^)]*\)/g,' '));
798:       if(outer)terms.push(outer);

1707: function restoredRuralRiSchedule(type,ctx){

1709:   if(!RURAL_INFERRED_ROUTE_DISTRICTS.has(district))return null;

949: function openDongRouteCache(){
950:   return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{

952:     const req=indexedDB.open(DONG_ROUTE_CACHE_DB,1);
953:     req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{

956:         db.createObjectStore(DONG_ROUTE_CACHE_STORE,{keyPath:'key'});

963: async function deleteCachedDongRoute(spec){

965:     const db=await openDongRouteCache();
966:     if(!db)return;

971:       tx.onerror=()=>resolve();

1552: function geoPointKm(lat,lng){
1553:   return {x:+lng*88,y:+lat*111};
1554: }
1555: function pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,a,b){
1556:   if(!a||!b)return Infinity;
1557:   const p=geoPointKm(lat,lng), p1=geoPointKm(a.lat,a.lng), p2=geoPointKm(b.lat,b.lng);
1558:   const vx=p2.x-p1.x, vy=p2.y-p1.y;
1559:   const wx=p.x-p1.x, wy=p.y-p1.y;
1560:   const vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
1561:   let t=vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0;
1562:   t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
1563:   return Math.hypot(p.x-(p1.x+t*vx),p.y-(p1.y+t*vy));
1564: }

1590: async function mapWithConcurrency(items,limit,worker){
1591:   const out=new Array(items.length);
1592:   let cursor=0;
1593:   async function run(){
1594:     while(true){
1595:       const i=cursor++;
1596:       if(i>=items.length)return;
1597:       try{out[i]=await worker(items[i],i)}
1598:       catch(e){console.warn('인접 수거노선 준비 실패',items[i],e);out[i]=null}
1599:     }
1600:   }
1601:   await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(limit,items.length)},()=>run()));
1602:   return out;
1603: }

1610: function extractAddressRoadNames(address){
1611:   const src=String(address||'').replace(/[(),]/g,' ');
1612:   const out=[];
1613:   const push=v=>{
1614:     const s=String(v||'').trim();
1615:     if(!s||out.some(x=>routeNorm(x)===routeNorm(s)))return;
1616:     out.push(s);
1617:   };

1624:   return out;

1637: function roadMatchesRouteRaw(road,raw){

1754: async function nearestRouteMatchesForAddress(type,ctx){
1755:   const district=canonicalDistrict(ctx?.legalEmd||ctx?.district||'');
1756:   if(!DONG_ROUTE_DISTRICTS.has(district)&&!RURAL_INFERRED_ROUTE_DISTRICTS.has(district))return null;
1757:   if(!Number.isFinite(+ctx.lat)||!Number.isFinite(+ctx.lng))return null;
1758: 
1759:   const specs=routeSpecsForDistrict(type,district);
1760:   if(!specs.length)return [];
1761: 
1762:   const items=await ensureDistrictRouteItems(type,district);
1763:   const itemMap=new Map(items.map(item=>[`${item.vehicle}|${item.day}`,item]));
1764:   const addressRoads=extractAddressRoadNames(ctx.address);
1765: 
1766:   const maxRouteKm=routeMatchLimitKm(district);
1767:   const measures=specs.map(spec=>{
1768:     const item=itemMap.get(`${spec.vehicle}|${spec.day}`)||null;
1769:     const distanceKm=item?routeItemDistanceKm(item,+ctx.lat,+ctx.lng):Infinity;
1770:     const roads=matchedAddressRoads(spec,addressRoads);
1771:     return {spec,item,distanceKm,roads,roadHit:roads.length>0};
1772:   });
1773: 
1774:   // 주소점에서 실제 표시 수거선까지 동지역 20m, 내수·북이 30m 이내인 노선만 후보로 인정합니다.
1775:   const eligible=measures.filter(m=>Number.isFinite(m.distanceKm)&&m.distanceKm<=maxRouteKm);
1776:   if(!eligible.length)return [];
1777: 
1778:   // 1순위: 허용거리 이내이면서 주소 도로명도 원본 코스에 직접 일치.
1779:   const exact=eligible.filter(m=>m.roadHit);
1780:   if(exact.length){
1781:     const byVehicle=new Map();
1782:     for(const m of exact){
1783:       if(!byVehicle.has(m.spec.vehicle))byVehicle.set(m.spec.vehicle,[]);
1784:       byVehicle.get(m.spec.vehicle).push(m);
1785:     }
1786:     const ranked=[...byVehicle.entries()].map(([vehicle,rows])=>({
1787:       vehicle,
1788:       rows,
1789:       minDist:Math.min(...rows.map(r=>r.distanceKm)),
1790:       roadHits:new Set(rows.flatMap(r=>r.roads)).size
1791:     })).sort((a,b)=>
1792:       a.minDist-b.minDist ||
1793:       b.roadHits-a.roadHits ||
1794:       b.rows.length-a.rows.length ||
1795:       a.vehicle.localeCompare(b.vehicle,'ko')
1796:     );
1797: 
1798:     const winner=ranked[0];
1799:     const winnerRoads=[...new Set(winner.rows.flatMap(r=>r.roads))];
1800:     // 동일 차량이라도 허용거리 안에 실제 선이 있는 요일만 일정에 포함합니다.
1801:     const selected=eligible.filter(m=>m.spec.vehicle===winner.vehicle);
1802:     return buildVehicleScheduleMatch(type,winner.vehicle,selected,'road',winnerRoads);
1803:   }
1804: 
1805:   // 2순위: 도로명 직접 일치가 없어도 허용거리 이내 실제 수거선 중 가장 가까운 차량.
1806:   eligible.sort((a,b)=>a.distanceKm-b.distanceKm);
1807:   const nearest=eligible[0];
1808:   const selected=eligible.filter(m=>m.spec.vehicle===nearest.spec.vehicle);
1809:   return buildVehicleScheduleMatch(type,nearest.spec.vehicle,selected,'distance',[]);
1810: }

1825: function isGenericRiDescriptor(desc,riName){
1826:   const n=routeNorm(desc?.term||'');
1827:   const r=routeNorm(riName||'');
1828:   if(!n||!r)return false;
1829:   return n===r || n===routeNorm(String(riName||'').replace(/리$/,''));
1830: }
1831: function riCandidateRouteDays(type,district,riName){
1832:   const schedule=riScheduleFromSources(type,district,riName,'');
1833:   const out=[];
1834:   for(const day of DAYS){
1835:     for(const p of (schedule?.[day]||[])){
1836:       if(!/^\d+$/.test(String(p.key||'')))continue;
1837:       const route=(DIRECT_ROUTES[type]||[]).find(r=>r.vehicle===p.key);
1838:       if(!route?.days?.[day])continue;
1839:       out.push({type,vehicle:p.key,day,raw:route.days[day],district,riName});
1840:     }
1841:   }
1842:   return out;
1843: }
1844: function descriptorWindowForRi(raw,riName){
1845:   const desc=routeDescriptorsForMap(raw);
1846:   const hits=[];
1847:   desc.forEach((d,i)=>{if(isGenericRiDescriptor(d,riName))hits.push(i)});
1848:   const picked=[];
1849:   const add=d=>{
1850:     if(!d)return;
1851:     const key=`${routeNorm(d.term)}|${d.scope||''}`;
1852:     if(!picked.some(x=>x._key===key))picked.push({...d,_key:key});
1853:   };
1854:   if(hits.length){
1855:     for(const idx of hits){

2770: function compactOrderedRuralAnchors(points,maxPts=12){
2771:   const clean=[];
2772:   for(const p of (points||[])){
2773:     if(!p||!Number.isFinite(+p.lat)||!Number.isFinite(+p.lng))continue;
2774:     const q={lat:+p.lat,lng:+p.lng,name:p.name||'',term:p.term||'',district:p.district||routePointAnyDistrict(p)||''};
2775:     if(clean.length&&routePointDistance(clean[clean.length-1],q)<0.05)continue;
2776:     clean.push(q);
2777:   }
2778:   if(clean.length<=maxPts)return clean;
2779:   const out=[];
2780:   for(let i=0;i<maxPts;i++){
2781:     const idx=Math.round(i*(clean.length-1)/(maxPts-1));
2782:     if(!out.length||idx!==out[out.length-1]._idx)out.push({...clean[idx],_idx:idx});
2783:   }
2784:   return out.map(({_idx,...p})=>p);
2785: }

293:   });

297:   if(!days.length)return '';

304: function contractExactScheduleMatch(type,ctx){
305:   const district=canonicalDistrict(ctx?.legalEmd||ctx?.district||'');
306:   const target=contractAddressNorm(ctx?.address||'');
307:   if(!target)return null;
308:   let best=null,bestLen=0;
309: 
310:   // 대행업체 원본 차량×요일 노선표의 주소목록을 직접 검색주소와 대조합니다.
311:   // 별도 특수지점만 보던 기존 로직 때문에 원본 일정이 있어도 '요일 확인 필요'로 빠지던 문제를 보완합니다.
312:   for(const r of (CONTRACT_ROUTES[type]||[])){
313:     if(!(r.districts||[]).some(d=>canonicalDistrict(d)===district))continue;
314:     let localBestLen=0,matchedAddress='';
315:     const matchedDays=[];
316:     for(const [day,raw] of Object.entries(r.days||{})){
317:       for(const part of String(raw||'').split(/[,/]/)){
318:         const key=contractAddressNorm(part);
319:         if(key.length<5)continue;
320:         const score=Math.min(key.length,target.length);
321:         if((target.includes(key)||key.includes(target))&&score>=5){
322:           if(!matchedDays.includes(day))matchedDays.push(day);
323:           if(score>localBestLen){localBestLen=score;matchedAddress=part.trim()}
324:         }
325:       }
326:     }
327:     if(matchedDays.length&&localBestLen>bestLen){
328:       const meta=contractRouteMeta(r.vehicle)||{};
329:       best={
330:         provider:r.provider||meta.provider||'',
331:         vehicle:r.plate||meta.plate||r.vehicle||'',
332:         days:DAYS.filter(d=>matchedDays.includes(d)),
333:         address:matchedAddress,
334:         housing:r.housing||meta.housing||'single',
335:         type
336:       };
337:       bestLen=localBestLen;
338:     }
339:   }
340: 
341:   // 특수주소 직접매칭은 그대로 유지하고, 더 구체적인 주소가 있으면 우선합니다.
342:   const rows=[];
343:   if(type==='general')rows.push(...CONTRACT_APARTMENTS.map(x=>({...x,housing:'apartment',type:'general'})));
344:   rows.push(...CONTRACT_SPECIAL_POINTS.filter(x=>x.type===type).map(x=>({...x,housing:'single'})));
345:   for(const x of rows){
346:     if(canonicalDistrict(x.district)!==district)continue;
347:     for(const key of contractAddressCandidates(x.address)){
348:       const score=Math.min(key.length,target.length);
349:       if((target.includes(key)||key.includes(target))&&score>bestLen){best=x;bestLen=score}
350:     }
351:   }
352:   return best;
353: }

1552: function geoPointKm(lat,lng){
1553:   return {x:+lng*88,y:+lat*111};
1554: }
1555: function pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,a,b){
1556:   if(!a||!b)return Infinity;
1557:   const p=geoPointKm(lat,lng), p1=geoPointKm(a.lat,a.lng), p2=geoPointKm(b.lat,b.lng);
1558:   const vx=p2.x-p1.x, vy=p2.y-p1.y;
1559:   const wx=p.x-p1.x, wy=p.y-p1.y;
1560:   const vv=vx*vx+vy*vy;
1561:   let t=vv>0?(wx*vx+wy*vy)/vv:0;
1562:   t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));
1563:   return Math.hypot(p.x-(p1.x+t*vx),p.y-(p1.y+t*vy));
1564: }

1567: function routeItemDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1568:   // 주소 20m 직접매칭은 실제 표시 수거선만 사용합니다.
1569:   let best=Infinity;
1570:   for(const seg of (item?.segments||[])){
1571:     for(let i=1;i<seg.length;i++){
1572:       best=Math.min(best,pointSegmentDistanceKm(lat,lng,seg[i-1],seg[i]));
1573:     }
1574:   }
1575:   return best;
1576: }
1577: function serviceZoneEvidenceDistanceKm(item,lat,lng){
1578:   // ① 수거권역 추정에는 선으로 만들기 어려운 시설·마을명도 '숨은 근거점'으로 활용합니다.
1579:   // 이 점들은 지도에 원/마커로 표시하지 않으며, 주소 20m 직접매칭에도 사용하지 않습니다.
1580:   let best=routeItemDistanceKm(item,lat,lng);

1590: async function mapWithConcurrency(items,limit,worker){
1591:   const out=new Array(items.length);
1592:   let cursor=0;
1593:   async function run(){
1594:     while(true){
1595:       const i=cursor++;
1596:       if(i>=items.length)return;
1597:       try{out[i]=await worker(items[i],i)}
1598:       catch(e){console.warn('인접 수거노선 준비 실패',items[i],e);out[i]=null}
1599:     }
1600:   }
1601:   await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(limit,items.length)},()=>run()));
1602:   return out;
1603: }

1754: async function nearestRouteMatchesForAddress(type,ctx){
1755:   const district=canonicalDistrict(ctx?.legalEmd||ctx?.district||'');
1756:   if(!DONG_ROUTE_DISTRICTS.has(district)&&!RURAL_INFERRED_ROUTE_DISTRICTS.has(district))return null;

1764:   const addressRoads=extractAddressRoadNames(ctx.address);
1765: 
1766:   const maxRouteKm=routeMatchLimitKm(district);
1767:   const measures=specs.map(spec=>{
1768:     const item=itemMap.get(`${spec.vehicle}|${spec.day}`)||null;
1769:     const distanceKm=item?routeItemDistanceKm(item,+ctx.lat,+ctx.lng):Infinity;
1770:     const roads=matchedAddressRoads(spec,addressRoads);
1771:     return {spec,item,distanceKm,roads,roadHit:roads.length>0};
1772:   });
1773: 
1774:   // 주소점에서 실제 표시 수거선까지 동지역 20m, 내수·북이 30m 이내인 노선만 후보로 인정합니다.
1775:   const eligible=measures.filter(m=>Number.isFinite(m.distanceKm)&&m.distanceKm<=maxRouteKm);
1776:   if(!eligible.length)return [];
1777: 
1778:   // 1순위: 허용거리 이내이면서 주소 도로명도 원본 코스에 직접 일치.
1779:   const exact=eligible.filter(m=>m.roadHit);
1780:   if(exact.length){
1781:     const byVehicle=new Map();
1782:     for(const m of exact){
1783:       if(!byVehicle.has(m.spec.vehicle))byVehicle.set(m.spec.vehicle,[]);
1784:       byVehicle.get(m.spec.vehicle).push(m);
1785:     }
1786:     const ranked=[...byVehicle.entries()].map(([vehicle,rows])=>({
1787:       vehicle,
1788:       rows,
1789:       minDist:Math.min(...rows.map(r=>r.distanceKm)),
1790:       roadHits:new Set(rows.flatMap(r=>r.roads)).size
1791:     })).sort((a,b)=>
1792:       a.minDist-b.minDist ||
1793:       b.roadHits-a.roadHits ||
1794:       b.rows.length-a.rows.length ||
1795:       a.vehicle.localeCompare(b.vehicle,'ko')
1796:     );
1797: 
1798:     const winner=ranked[0];
1799:     const winnerRoads=[...new Set(winner.rows.flatMap(r=>r.roads))];
1800:     // 동일 차량이라도 허용거리 안에 실제 선이 있는 요일만 일정에 포함합니다.
1801:     const selected=eligible.filter(m=>m.spec.vehicle===winner.vehicle);
1802:     return buildVehicleScheduleMatch(type,winner.vehicle,selected,'road',winnerRoads);
1803:   }

3153: function featureOuterPathPoints(feature){

3156: function wholeFeatureZone(type,feature,meta){
3157:   const g=feature?.geometry;if(!g)return;
3158:   meta={...meta,rural:meta.rural??RI_LAYER_DISTRICTS.has(meta.district||'')};
3159:   const polys=g.type==='Polygon'?[g.coordinates]:(g.type==='MultiPolygon'?g.coordinates:[]);
3160:   polys.forEach(poly=>{const ring=poly?.[0]||[];if(ring.length>=3)addServiceZonePolygon(type,ring.map(([lng,lat])=>({lat,lng})),meta)});
3161: }
3162: function classifyServiceGroups(groups,lat,lng){
3163:   const ranked=(groups||[]).map(g=>({group:g,distanceKm:serviceGroupDistanceKm(g,lat,lng)})).filter(x=>Number.isFinite(x.distanceKm)).sort((a,b)=>a.distanceKm-b.distanceKm);
3164:   if(!ranked.length)return null;
3165:   const best=ranked[0],second=ranked[1]||null;
3166:   const delta=second?second.distanceKm-best.distanceKm:Infinity;
3167:   const ambiguous=!!second && (delta<0.08 || (best.distanceKm>0.05 && second.distanceKm/(best.distanceKm||.001)<1.18));
3168:   return {...best,second,ambiguous};
3169: }
3170: function mergedRowRuns(cells){
3171:   const runs=[];let run=null;
3172:   for(const c of cells){
3173:     const sig=`${c.key}|${c.estimated?'E':(c.ambiguous?'A':'C')}`;
3174:     if(!run||run.sig!==sig||c.ix!==run.x1+1){
3175:       if(run)runs.push(run);
3176:       run={...c,sig,x0:c.ix,x1:c.ix,neighborSupport:+c.neighborSupport||0,neighborTotal:+c.neighborTotal||0};
3177:     }else{
3178:       run.x1=c.ix;
3179:       if(run.estimated){
3180:         run.neighborSupport+=(+c.neighborSupport||0);
3181:         run.neighborTotal+=(+c.neighborTotal||0);
3182:         run.neighborRatio=run.neighborTotal?run.neighborSupport/run.neighborTotal:(+run.neighborRatio||0);
3183:       }
3184:     }
3185:   }
3186:   if(run)runs.push(run);return runs;
3187: }
3188: function contractorRiScheduleFromSources(type,district,riName,riCode=''){
3189:   const byDay={};
3190:   const candidates=normalizedRiTextCandidates(riName,riCode).concat(extraRiTextAliases(district,riName)).filter(Boolean);
3191:   const contractRi=isContractLayerRi(district,riName);
3192:   for(const route of contractorZoneRoutes(type,district)){
3193:     if(route.housing==='apartment')continue;
3194:     for(const [day,raw] of Object.entries(route.days||{})){
3195:       const nraw=routeNorm(raw);
3196:       let hit=candidates.some(x=>x&&nraw.includes(x));
3197:       // 오창산업단지는 주소표현이 '과학산업/2산단/중심상업/택지' 등으로 적혀
3198:       // 법정리명이 생략된 행이 많으므로, 사용자가 지정한 대행 리에는 산업단지 노선의 요일을 함께 묶습니다.
3199:       if(!hit&&contractRi){
3200:         hit=OCHANG_SCIENCE_ROAD_HINTS.some(t=>nraw.includes(routeNorm(t)))||nraw.includes(routeNorm('2산단'))||nraw.includes(routeNorm('산단'));
3201:       }
3202:       if(hit)addProviderToSchedule(byDay,day,route.vehicle);
3203:     }
3204:   }
3205:   // 산업단지 대행 리인데 법정리명이 전혀 적히지 않은 노선도 있으므로
3206:   // 해당 리에 특정 일자가 하나도 잡히지 않으면 오창 단독주택 대행노선의 실제 운행요일을 사용합니다.
3207:   if(contractRi&&!Object.keys(byDay).length){
3208:     for(const route of contractorZoneRoutes(type,district)){
3209:       if(route.housing==='apartment')continue;
3210:       for(const day of Object.keys(route.days||{}))addProviderToSchedule(byDay,day,route.vehicle);
3211:     }
3212:   }
3213:   DAYS.forEach(day=>{if(byDay[day])byDay[day].sort((a,b)=>a.key.localeCompare(b.key,'ko'))});
3214:   return byDay;
3215: }

3217: function clipRoutePathToFeature(path,feature){
3218:   const dense=densifyRoutePath(path,0.025),parts=[];let current=[];
3219:   for(const p of dense){
3220:     if(pointInGeoFeature([+p.lng,+p.lat],feature))current.push({lat:+p.lat,lng:+p.lng});
3221:     else{if(current.length>=2)parts.push(current);current=[]}
3222:   }
3223:   if(current.length>=2)parts.push(current);
3224:   return parts;
3225: }
3226: function thinRoutePath(path,maxPts=90){
3227:   const pts=(path||[]).filter(p=>Number.isFinite(+p.lat)&&Number.isFinite(+p.lng));
3228:   if(pts.length<=maxPts)return pts.map(p=>({lat:+p.lat,lng:+p.lng}));
3229:   const out=[];
3230:   for(let i=0;i<maxPts;i++){
3231:     const idx=Math.round(i*(pts.length-1)/(maxPts-1));
3232:     if(!out.length||idx!==out[out.length-1]._idx)out.push({lat:+pts[idx].lat,lng:+pts[idx].lng,_idx:idx});
3233:   }
3234:   return out.map(({_idx,...p})=>p);
3235: }
3236: function bufferedRoutePolygon(path,radiusKm=RURAL_ROUTE_MATCH_KM){
3237:   const pts=thinRoutePath(path,90);if(pts.length<2)return [];
3238:   const left=[],right=[];
3239:   for(let i=0;i<pts.length;i++){
3240:     const a=pts[Math.max(0,i-1)],b=pts[Math.min(pts.length-1,i+1)];
3241:     const dx=(+b.lng-+a.lng)*88,dy=(+b.lat-+a.lat)*111,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
3242:     const nx=-dy/len,ny=dx/len;
3243:     left.push({lat:+pts[i].lat+(ny*radiusKm/111),lng:+pts[i].lng+(nx*radiusKm/88)});
3244:     right.push({lat:+pts[i].lat-(ny*radiusKm/111),lng:+pts[i].lng-(nx*radiusKm/88)});
3245:   }
3246:   return [...left,...right.reverse()];
3247: }
3248: async function buildRuralRouteBufferZones(type,region){
3249:   const groups=await serviceRouteGroups(type,region.district,region.riName||'');
3250:   const usable=groups.filter(g=>(g.items||[]).some(item=>(item.segments||[]).length));
3251:   let added=0;
3252:   for(const group of usable){
3253:     for(const item of (group.items||[])){
3254:       for(const seg of (item.segments||[])){
3255:         for(const part of clipRoutePathToFeature(seg,region.feature)){
3256:           const poly=bufferedRoutePolygon(part,RURAL_ROUTE_MATCH_KM);if(poly.length<4)continue;
3257:           addServiceZonePolygon(type,poly,{
3258:             district:region.district,riName:region.riName||'',vehicle:group.vehicle,provider:group.provider||routeCompany(group.vehicle),plate:group.plate||routePlate(group.vehicle),
3259:             days:group.days,groupKey:`30m|${region.district}|${region.riName||''}|${group.key}|${added}`,automatic:true,rural:true,routeBuffer:true,riGrouped:true,contractLayer:false
3260:           });
3261:           added++;
3262:         }
3263:       }
3264:     }
